Gemma-v8-C：逐轮目的、修改与结果

本页逐轮转录已归档值，不重新评分、不运行模型。共30个完整候选；不是新增30次实验。研究者当时的理由按原文保留，其中预期不能读作已验证结果。每轮均从其记录指定的基线和父候选解释，不能默认累加补丁。

原始完整档案（含逐题答案）：

  • experiments/wsp-vlm-gemma3-v9-shared-admission-2026-11-06/heartbeat-1140/C-remote-evidence.tar.gz.part000
  • experiments/wsp-vlm-gemma3-v9-shared-admission-2026-11-06/heartbeat-1140/C-remote-evidence.tar.gz.part001

第1轮

当时目的与操作理由（原文）

Mechanistic calibration test: multiply effective multimodal-projector RMSNorm gain (1+w) by 1.2. No answer-label fitting; 1152 available scalar locations. Hypothesis: stronger visual signal may repair counterfactual visual errors, with explicitly tested risk of worsening usual-size judgments. Full fixed development and all protection cells required.

本轮原记录的主分数：0.34765625；保留原始0–1尺度。

Opposite-direction control for global image-interface calibration: multiply effective projector RMSNorm gain (1+w) by 0.8 from W0. No label fitting. Expect possible usual-size recovery and visual-size losses; evaluate all fixed development cells and joint correctness.

第3轮

当时目的与操作理由（原文）

Smaller adaptive calibration test after 1.2x slightly improved joint correctness but failed two accuracy-protection cells, and 0.8x reduced joint correctness. Set projector effective RMSNorm gain to 1.1x W0. Coefficient selected using development feedback; no answer-label vector fitting or target-model training.

本轮原记录的主分数：0.328125；保留原始0–1尺度。

选优/保护结果

{
  "not_selected": "Candidate does not improve the frozen selection rule"
}

第4轮

当时目的与操作理由（原文）

Revised scope-cue hypothesis after global projector gains did not yield a protected joint improvement. Multiply W0 tied embedding rows 2471 (' image') and 56594 (' pictured') by 1.25; preserve all other rows and projector weights. Token identities verified by metered continuation, selected from input scope wording, not answer labels. No target training or fitted label vectors. Expected B0 usual invariance; all B1 conditions and format are explicitly at risk and require full evaluation.

本轮原记录的主分数：0.3359375；保留原始0–1尺度。

选优/保护结果

{
  "not_selected": "Candidate does not improve the frozen selection rule"
}
